- **paper-world**: Turn a frame of real footage into a dimensional paper-collage world and back - freeze on a word, cut the subject out as real paper, let the background fall away like a stage flat, climb through a parallax paper city and paper clouds (Blender headless), stage a literal stat or a brand reveal, then return to footage through a cloud. Use for collage / paper-craft / cut-out beats inside a live-action video (stat visualisations, metaphors, logo reveals), or any shot where footage becomes an illustrated 3D world.
